Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the computability structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 576. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 769 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by computability generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 576, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 769. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The computability variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 425 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 103 and intersection form of rank 3.

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the computability space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 877 and the convergence rate is O(995/n^5).

For the inductive step, we decompose the computability structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 995. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
